## 3. COL-to-World Coordinate & Floor `Z` Guide

1. Every `.col.jsonl` line has `{ index, name, modelId, rad, center, min, max, spheres, boxes, verts, faces }` in **local model space**.
2. Note that `modelId` inside GTA III `.col` headers is a stale export constant (`47`); the engine binds collisions to `.ide` models **by `name` (case-insensitive)**.
3. For identity-rotation placements (`0, 0, 0, 1` — used by nearly all buildings and road tiles), world coordinates are simply `world = inst.pos + local`.
4. For rotated placements (`qx, qy, qz, qw`), GTA III constructs the rotation matrix using the **conjugate** quaternion `(-qx, -qy, -qz, qw)` after applying `scale`.
5. When finding exact ground/roof/pool/step heights, query the specific `boxes`, `spheres`, or `verts`/`faces` in the model's `.col.jsonl` entry rather than relying solely on the outer `min/max` AABB.
